NULL;\n491\t    }\n492\t\n493\t    if (unlikely(IPV4_GET_RAW_IPLEN(ip4h) < IPV4_GET_RAW_HLEN(ip4h))) {\n494\t        ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_IPLEN_SMALLER_THAN_HLEN);\n495\t        return NULL;\n496\t    }\n497\t\n498\t    if (unlikely(len < IPV4_GET_RAW_IPLEN(ip4h))) {\n499\t        ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_TRUNC_PKT);\n500\t        return NULL;\n501\t    }\n502\t\n503\t    /* set the address struct */\n504\t    SET_IPV4_SRC_ADDR(ip4h, &p->src);\n505\t    SET_IPV4_DST_ADDR(ip4h, &p->dst);\n506\t\n507\t    /* save the options len */\n508\t    uint8_t ip_opt_len = IPV4_GET_RAW_HLEN(ip4h) - IPV4_HEADER_LEN;\n509\t    if (ip_opt_len > 0) {\n510\t        IPV4Options opts;\n511\t        memset(&opts, 0x00, sizeof(opts));\n512\t        if (DecodeIPV4Options(p, pkt + IPV4_HEADER_LEN, ip_opt_len, &opts) < 0) {\n513\t            return NULL;\n514\t        }\n515\t    }\n516\t\n517\t    return ip4h;\n518\t}\n519\t\n520\tint DecodeIPV4(ThreadVars *tv, DecodeThreadVars *dtv, Packet *p,\n521\t        const uint8_t *pkt, uint16_t len)\n522\t{\n523\t    StatsIncr(tv, dtv->counter_ipv4);\n524\t\n525\t    SCLogDebug(\"pkt %p len %\"PRIu16\"\", pkt, len);\n526\t\n527\t    if (!PacketIncreaseCheckLayers(p)) {\n528\t        return TM_ECODE_FAILED;\n529\t    }\n530\t    /* do the actual decoding */\n531\t    const IPV4Hdr *ip4h = DecodeIPV4Packet(p, pkt, len);\n532\t    if (unlikely(ip4h == NULL)) {\n533\t        SCLogDebug(\"decoding IPv4 packet failed\");\n534\t        PacketClearL3(p);\n535\t        return TM_ECODE_FAILED;\n536\t    }\n537\t    p->proto = IPV4_GET_RAW_IPPROTO(ip4h);\n538\t\n539\t    /* If a fragment, pass off for re-assembly. */\n540\t    if (unlikely(IPV4_GET_RAW_FRAGOFFSET(ip4h) > 0 || IPV4_GET_RAW_FLAG_MF(ip4h))) {\n541\t        Packet *rp = Defrag(tv, dtv, p);\n542\t        if (rp != NULL) {\n543\t            PacketEnqueueNoLock(&tv->decode_pq, rp);\n544\t        }\n545\t        p->flags |= PKT_IS_FRAGMENT;\n546\t        return TM_ECODE_OK;\n547\t    }\n548\t\n549\t    /* do hdr test, process hdr rules */\n550\t\n551\t#ifdef DEBUG\n552\t    if (SCLogDebugEnabled()) { /* only convert the addresses if debug is really enabled */\n553\t        /* debug print */\n554\t        char s[16], d[16];\n555\t        PrintInet(AF_INET, (const void *)GET_IPV4_SRC_ADDR_PTR(p), s, sizeof(s));\n556\t        PrintInet(AF_INET, (const void *)GET_IPV4_DST_ADDR_PTR(p), d, sizeof(d));\n557\t        SCLogDebug(\"IPV4 %s->%s PROTO: %\" PRIu32 \" OFFSET: %\" PRIu32 \" RF: %\" PRIu8 \" DF: %\" PRIu8\n558\t                   \" MF: %\" PRIu8 \" ID: %\" PRIu32 \"\",\n559\t                s, d, IPV4_GET_RAW_IPPROTO(ip4h), IPV4_GET_RAW_IPOFFSET(ip4h),\n560\t                IPV4_GET_RAW_FLAG_RF(ip4h), IPV4_GET_RAW_FLAG_DF(ip4h), IPV4_GET_RAW_FLAG_MF(ip4h),\n561\t                IPV4_GET_RAW_IPID(ip4h));\n562\t    }\n563\t#endif /* DEBUG */\n564\t\n565\t    const uint8_t *data = pkt + IPV4_GET_RAW_HLEN(ip4h);\n566\t    const uint16_t data_len = IPV4_GET_RAW_IPLEN(ip4h) - IPV4_GET_RAW_HLEN(ip4h);\n567\t\n568\t    /* check what next decoder to invoke */\n569\t    switch (p->proto) {\n570\t        case IPPROTO_TCP:\n571\t            DecodeTCP(tv, dtv, p, data, data_len);\n572\t            break;\n573\t        case IPPROTO_UDP:\n574\t            DecodeUDP(tv, dtv, p, data, data_len);\n575\t            break;\n576\t        case IPPROTO_ICMP:\n577\t            DecodeICMPV4(tv, dtv, p, data, data_len);\n578\t            break;\n579\t        case IPPROTO_GRE:\n580\t            DecodeGRE(tv, dtv, p, data, data_len);\n581\t            break;\n582\t        case IPPROTO_SCTP:\n583\t            DecodeSCTP(tv, dtv, p, data, data_len);\n584\t            break;\n585\t        case IPPROTO_ESP:\n586\t            DecodeESP(tv, dtv, p, data, data_len);\n587\t            break;\n588\t        case IPPROTO_IPV6: {\n589\t            /* spawn off tunnel packet */\n590\t            Packet *tp = PacketTunnelPktSetup(tv, dtv, p, data, data_len, DECODE_TUNNEL_IPV6);\n591\t            if (tp != NULL) {\n592\t                PKT_SET_SRC(tp, PKT_SRC_DECODER_IPV4);\n593\t                PacketEnqueueNoLock(&tv->decode_pq, tp);\n594\t                StatsIncr(tv, dtv->counter_ipv6inipv4);\n595\t            }\n596\t            FlowSetupPacket(p);\n597\t            break;\n598\t        }\n599\t        case IPPROTO_IPIP: {\n600\t            /* spawn off tunnel packet */\n601\t            Packet *tp = PacketTunnelPktSetup(tv, dtv, p, data, data_len, DECODE_TUNNEL_IPV4);\n602\t            if (tp != NULL) {\n603\t                PKT_SET_SRC(tp, PKT_SRC_DECODER_IPV4);\n604\t                PacketEnqueueNoLock(&tv->decode_pq, tp);\n605\t                StatsIncr(tv, dtv->counter_ipv4inipv4);\n606\t            }\n607\t            FlowSetupPacket(p);\n608\t            break;\n609\t        }\n610\t        case IPPROTO_IP:\n611\t            /* check PPP VJ uncompressed packets and decode tcp dummy */\n612\t            if (p->flags & PKT_PPP_VJ_UCOMP) {\n613\t                DecodeTCP(tv, dtv, p, data, data_len);\n614\t            }\n615\t            break;\n616\t        case IPPROTO_ICMPV6:\n617\t            ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_WITH_ICMPV6);\n618\t            break;\n619\t    }\n620\t\n621\t    return TM_ECODE_OK;\n622\t}\n623\t\n624\t/* UNITTESTS */\n625\t#ifdef UNITTESTS\n626\t#include Software\n * Foundation, Inc., 51 Franklin Street, Fifth Floor, Boston, MA\n * 02110-1301, USA.\n */\n\n/**\n * \\ingroup decode\n *\n * @{\n */\n\n\n/**\n * \\file\n *\n * \\author Victor Julien <victor@inliniac.net>\n * \\author Brian Rectanus <brectanu@gmail.com>\n *\n * Decode IPv4\n */\n\n#include \"suricata-common.h\"\n#include \"decode-ipv4.h\"\n#include \"decode.h\"\n#include \"defrag.h\"\n#include \"flow.h\"\n#include \"util-print.h\"\n\n/* Generic validation\n *\n * [--type--][--len---]\n *\n * \\todo This function needs removed in favor of specific validation.\n *\n * See: RFC 791\n */\nstatic int IPV4OptValidateGeneric(Packet *p, const IPV4Opt *o)\n{\n    switch (o->type) {\n        /* See: RFC 4782 */\n        case IPV4_OPT_QS:\n            if (o->len < IPV4_OPT_QS_MIN) {\n                ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_INVALID_LEN);\n                return -1;\n            }\n            break;\n        /* See: RFC 1108 */\n        case IPV4_OPT_SEC:\n        case IPV4_OPT_ESEC:\n            if (unlikely(o->len < IPV4_OPT_SEC_MIN)) {\n                ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_INVALID_LEN);\n                return -1;\n            }\n            break;\n        case IPV4_OPT_SID:\n            if (o->len != IPV4_OPT_SID_LEN) {\n                ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_INVALID_LEN);\n                return -1;\n            }\n            break;\n        /* See: RFC 2113 */\n        case IPV4_OPT_RTRALT:\n            if (o->len != IPV4_OPT_RTRALT_LEN) {\n                ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_INVALID_LEN);\n                return -1;\n            }\n            break;\n        default:\n            /* Should never get here unless there is a coding error */\n            ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_UNKNOWN);\n            return -1;\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/* Validate route type options\n *\n * [--type--][--len---][--ptr---][address1]...[addressN]\n *\n * See: RFC 791\n */\nstatic int IPV4OptValidateRoute(Packet *p, const IPV4Opt *o)\n{\n    uint8_t ptr;\n\n    /* Check length */\n    if (unlikely(o->len < IPV4_OPT_ROUTE_MIN)) {\n        ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_INVALID_LEN);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    /* Data is required */\n    if (unlikely(o->data == NULL)) {\n        ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_MALFORMED);\n        return -1;\n    }\n    ptr = *o->data;\n\n    /* Address pointer is 1 based and points at least after type+len+ptr,\n     * must be a incremented by 4 bytes (address size) and cannot extend\n     * past option length.\n     */\n    if (unlikely((ptr < 4) || (ptr % 4) || (ptr > o->len + 1))) {\n        ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_MALFORMED);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/* Validate timestamp type options\n *\n * [--type--][--len---][--ptr---][ovfl][flag][rec1----...]...[recN----...]\n * NOTE: rec could be 4 (ts only) or 8 (ip+ts) bytes in length.\n *\n * See: RFC 781\n */\nstatic int IPV4OptValidateTimestamp(Packet *p, const IPV4Opt *o)\n{\n    uint8_t ptr;\n    uint8_t flag;\n    uint8_t rec_size;\n\n    /* Check length */\n    if (unlikely(o->len < IPV4_OPT_TS_MIN)) {\n        ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_INVALID_LEN);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    /* Data is required */\n    if (unlikely(o->data == NULL)) {\n        ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_MALFORMED);\n        return -1;\n    }\n    ptr = *o->data;\n\n    /* We need the flag to determine what is in the option payload */\n    if (unlikely(ptr < 5)) {\n        ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_MALFORMED);\n        return -1;\n    }\n    flag = *(o->data + 1) & 0x0f;\n\n    /* A flag of 1|3 means we have both the ip+ts in each record */\n    rec_size = ((flag == 1) || (flag == 3)) ? 8 : 4;\n\n    /* Address pointer is 1 based and points at least after\n     * type+len+ptr+ovfl+flag, must be incremented by by the rec_size\n     * and cannot extend past option length.\n     */\n    if (unlikely(((ptr - 5) % rec_size) || (ptr > o->len + 1))) {\n        ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_MALFORMED);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/* Validate CIPSO option\n *\n * [--type--][--len---][--doi---][tags--...]\n *\n * See: draft-ietf-cipso-ipsecurity-01.txt\n * See: FIPS 188 (tags 6 & 7)\n */\nstatic int IPV4OptValidateCIPSO(Packet *p, const IPV4Opt *o)\n{\n//    uint32_t doi;\n    const uint8_t *tag;\n    uint16_t len;\n\n    /* Check length */\n    if (unlikely(o->len < IPV4_OPT_CIPSO_MIN)) {\n        ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_INVALID_LEN);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    /* Data is required */\n    if (unlikely(o->data == NULL)) {\n        ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_MALFORMED);\n        return -1;\n    }\n//    doi = *o->data;\n    tag = o->data + 4;\n    len = o->len - 1 - 1 - 4; /* Length of tags after header */\n\n\n#if 0\n    /* Domain of Interest (DOI) of 0 is reserved and thus invalid */\n    /** \\todo Apparently a DOI of zero is fine in practice - verify. */\n    if (doi == 0) {\n        ENGINE_SET_EVENT(p,IPV4_OPT_MALFORMED);\n        return -1;\n    }\n#endif\n\n    /* NOTE: We know len has passed min tests prior to this call */\n\n    /* Check that tags are formatted correctly\n     * [-ttype--][--tlen--][-tagdata-...]\n     */\n    while (len) {\n        uint8_t ttype;\n        uint8_t tlen;\n\n        /* Tag header must fit within option length */\n        if (unlikely(len < 2)) {\n            //printf(\"CIPSO tag header too large %\" PRIu16 \" < 2\\n\", len);\n            ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_MALFORMED);\n            return -1;\n        }\n\n        /* Tag header is type+len */\n        ttype = *(tag++);\n        tlen = *(tag++);\n\n        /* Tag length must fit within the option length */\n        if (unlikely(tlen > len)) {\n            //printf(\"CIPSO tag len too large %\" PRIu8 \" > %\" PRIu16 \"\\n\", tlen, len);\n            ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_MALFORMED);\n            return -1;\n        }\n\n        switch(ttype) {\n            case 1:\n            case 2:\n            case 5:\n            case 6:\n            case 7:\n                /* Tag is at least 4 and at most the remainder of option len */\n                if (unlikely((tlen < 4) || (tlen > len))) {\n                    //printf(\"CIPSO tag %\" PRIu8 \" bad tlen=%\" PRIu8 \" len=%\" PRIu8 \"\\n\", ttype, tlen, len);\n                    ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_MALFORMED);\n                    return -1;\n                }\n\n                /* The alignment octet is always 0 except tag\n                 * type 7, which has no such field.\n                 */\n                if (unlikely((ttype != 7) && (*tag != 0))) {\n                    //printf(\"CIPSO tag %\" PRIu8 \" ao=%\" PRIu8 \"\\n\", ttype, tlen);\n                    ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_MALFORMED);\n                    return -1;\n                }\n\n                /* Skip the rest of the tag payload */\n                tag += tlen - 2;\n                len -= tlen;\n\n                continue;\n            case 0:\n                /* Tag type 0 is reserved and thus invalid */\n                /** \\todo Wireshark marks this a padding, but spec says reserved. */\n                ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p,IPV4_OPT_MALFORMED);\n                return -1;\n            default:\n                //printf(\"CIPSO tag %\" PRIu8 \" unknown tag\\n\", ttype);\n                ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_MALFORMED);\n                /** \\todo May not want to return error here on unknown tag type (at least not for 3|4) */\n                return -1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n\ntypedef struct IPV4Options_ {\n    IPV4Opt o_rr;\n    IPV4Opt o_qs;\n    IPV4Opt o_ts;\n    IPV4Opt o_sec;\n    IPV4Opt o_lsrr;\n    IPV4Opt o_esec;\n    IPV4Opt o_cipso;\n    IPV4Opt o_sid;\n    IPV4Opt o_ssrr;\n    IPV4Opt o_rtralt;\n} IPV4Options;\n\n/**\n * Decode/Validate IPv4 Options.\n */\nstatic int DecodeIPV4Options(Packet *p, const uint8_t *pkt, uint16_t len, IPV4Options *opts)\n{\n    uint16_t plen = len;\n\n#ifdef DEBUG\n    if (SCLogDebugEnabled()) {\n        uint16_t i;\n        char buf[256] = \"\";\n        int offset = 0;\n\n        for (i = 0; i < len; i++) {\n            offset += snprintf(buf + offset, (sizeof(buf) - offset), \"%02\" PRIx8 \" \", pkt[i]);\n        }\n        SCLogDebug(\"IPV4OPTS: { %s}\", buf);\n    }\n#endif\n\n    /* Options length must be padded to 8byte boundary */\n    if (plen % 8) {\n        ENGINE_SET_EVENT(p,IPV4_OPT_PAD_REQUIRED);\n        /* Warn - we can keep going */\n    }\n\n    while (plen)\n    {\n        p->l3.vars.ip4.opt_cnt++;\n\n        /* single byte options */\n        if (*pkt == IPV4_OPT_EOL) {\n            /** \\todo What if more data exist after EOL (possible covert channel or data leakage)? */\n            SCLogDebug(\"IPV4OPT %\" PRIu8 \" len 1 @ %d/%d\",\n                   *pkt, (len - plen), (len - 1));\n            p->l3.vars.ip4.opts_set |= IPV4_OPT_FLAG_EOL;\n            break;\n        } else if (*pkt == IPV4_OPT_NOP) {\n            SCLogDebug(\"IPV4OPT %\" PRIu8 \" len 1 @ %d/%d\",\n                   *pkt, (len - plen), (len - 1));\n            pkt++;\n            plen--;\n\n            p->l3.vars.ip4.opts_set |= IPV4_OPT_FLAG_NOP;\n\n            /* multibyte options */\n        } else {\n            if (unlikely(plen < 2)) {\n                /** \\todo What if padding is non-zero (possible covert channel or data leakage)? */\n                /** \\todo Spec seems to indicate EOL required if there is padding */\n                ENGINE_SET_EVENT(p,IPV4_OPT_EOL_REQUIRED);\n                break;\n            }\n\n            /* Option length is too big for packet */\n            if (unlikely(*(pkt+1) > plen)) {\n                ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_INVALID_LEN);\n                return -1;\n            }\n\n            IPV4Opt opt = {*pkt, *(pkt+1), plen > 2 ? (pkt + 2) : NULL };\n\n            /* we already know that the total options len is valid,\n             * so here the len of the specific option must be bad.\n             * Also check for invalid lengths 0 and 1. */\n            if (unlikely(opt.len > plen || opt.len < 2)) {\n                ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_INVALID_LEN);\n                return -1;\n            }\n            /* we are parsing the most commonly used opts to prevent\n             * us from having to walk the opts list for these all the\n             * time. */\n            /** \\todo Figure out which IP options are more common and list them first */\n            switch (opt.type) {\n                case IPV4_OPT_TS:\n                    if (opts->o_ts.type != 0) {\n                        ENGINE_SET_EVENT(p,IPV4_OPT_DUPLICATE);\n                        /* Warn - we can keep going */\n                    } else if (IPV4OptValidateTimestamp(p, &opt) == 0) {\n                        opts->o_ts = opt;\n                        p->l3.vars.ip4.opts_set |= IPV4_OPT_FLAG_TS;\n                    }\n                    break;\n                case IPV4_OPT_RR:\n                    if (opts->o_rr.type != 0) {\n                        ENGINE_SET_EVENT(p,IPV4_OPT_DUPLICATE);\n                        /* Warn - we can keep going */\n                    } else if (IPV4OptValidateRoute(p, &opt) == 0) {\n                        opts->o_rr = opt;\n                        p->l3.vars.ip4.opts_set |= IPV4_OPT_FLAG_RR;\n                    }\n                    break;\n                case IPV4_OPT_QS:\n                    if (opts->o_qs.type != 0) {\n                        ENGINE_SET_EVENT(p,IPV4_OPT_DUPLICATE);\n                        /* Warn - we can keep going */\n                    } else if (IPV4OptValidateGeneric(p, &opt) == 0) {\n                        opts->o_qs = opt;\n                        p->l3.vars.ip4.opts_set |= IPV4_OPT_FLAG_QS;\n                    }\n                    break;\n                case IPV4_OPT_SEC:\n                    if (opts->o_sec.type != 0) {\n                        ENGINE_SET_EVENT(p,IPV4_OPT_DUPLICATE);\n                        /* Warn - we can keep going */\n                    } else if (IPV4OptValidateGeneric(p, &opt) == 0) {\n                        opts->o_sec = opt;\n                        p->l3.vars.ip4.opts_set |= IPV4_OPT_FLAG_SEC;\n                    }\n                    break;\n                case IPV4_OPT_LSRR:\n                    if (opts->o_lsrr.type != 0) {\n                        ENGINE_SET_EVENT(p,IPV4_OPT_DUPLICATE);\n                        /* Warn - we can keep going */\n                    } else if (IPV4OptValidateRoute(p, &opt) == 0) {\n                        opts->o_lsrr = opt;\n                        p->l3.vars.ip4.opts_set |= IPV4_OPT_FLAG_LSRR;\n                    }\n                    break;\n                case IPV4_OPT_ESEC:\n                    if (opts->o_esec.type != 0) {\n                        ENGINE_SET_EVENT(p, IPV4_OPT_DUPLICATE);\n                        /* Warn - we can keep going */\n                    } else if (IPV4OptValidateGeneric(p, &opt) == 0) {\n                        opts->o_esec = opt;\n                        p->l3.vars.ip4.opts_set |= IPV4_OPT_FLAG_ESEC;\n                    }\n                    break;\n                case IPV4_OPT_CIPSO:\n                    if (opts->o_cipso.type != 0) {\n                        ENGINE_SET_EVENT(p,IPV4_OPT_DUPLICATE);\n                        /* Warn - we can keep going */\n                    } else if (IPV4OptValidateCIPSO(p, &opt) == 0) {\n                        opts->o_cipso = opt;\n                        p->l3.vars.ip4.opts_set |= IPV4_OPT_FLAG_CIPSO;\n                    }\n                    break;\n                case IPV4_OPT_SID:\n                    if (opts->o_sid.type != 0) {\n                        ENGINE_SET_EVENT(p,IPV4_OPT_DUPLICATE);\n                        /* Warn - we can keep going */\n                    } else if (IPV4OptValidateGeneric(p, &opt) == 0) {\n                        opts->o_sid = opt;\n                        p->l3.vars.ip4.opts_set |= IPV4_OPT_FLAG_SID;\n                    }\n                    break;\n                case IPV4_OPT_SSRR:\n                    if (opts->o_ssrr.type != 0) {\n                        ENGINE_SET_EVENT(p,IPV4_OPT_DUPLICATE);\n                        /* Warn - we can keep going */\n                    } else if (IPV4OptValidateRoute(p, &opt) == 0) {\n                        opts->o_ssrr = opt;\n                        p->l3.vars.ip4.opts_set |= IPV4_OPT_FLAG_SSRR;\n                    }\n                    break;\n                case IPV4_OPT_RTRALT:\n                    if (opts->o_rtralt.type != 0) {\n                        ENGINE_SET_EVENT(p,IPV4_OPT_DUPLICATE);\n                        /* Warn - we can keep going */\n                    } else if (IPV4OptValidateGeneric(p, &opt) == 0) {\n                        opts->o_rtralt = opt;\n                        p->l3.vars.ip4.opts_set |= IPV4_OPT_FLAG_RTRALT;\n                    }\n                    break;\n                default:\n                    SCLogDebug(\"IPV4OPT <unknown> (%\" PRIu8 \") len %\" PRIu8,\n                           opt.type, opt.len);\n                    ENGINE_SET_EVENT(p,IPV4_OPT_INVALID);\n                    /* Warn - we can keep going */\n                    break;\n            }\n\n            pkt += opt.len;\n            plen -= opt.len;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n\nstatic const IPV4Hdr *DecodeIPV4Packet(Packet *p, const uint8_t *pkt, uint16_t len)\n{\n    if (unlikely(len < IPV4_HEADER_LEN)) {\n        ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_PKT_TOO_SMALL);\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    if (unlikely(IP_GET_RAW_VER(pkt) != 4)) {\n        SCLogDebug(\"wrong ip version %d\",IP_GET_RAW_VER(pkt));\n        ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_WRONG_IP_VER);\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    const IPV4Hdr *ip4h = PacketSetIPV4(p, pkt);\n\n    if (unlikely(IPV4_GET_RAW_HLEN(ip4h) < IPV4_HEADER_LEN)) {\n        ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_HLEN_TOO_SMALL);\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    if (unlikely(IPV4_GET_RAW_IPLEN(ip4h) < IPV4_GET_RAW_HLEN(ip4h))) {\n        ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_IPLEN_SMALLER_THAN_HLEN);\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    if (unlikely(len < IPV4_GET_RAW_IPLEN(ip4h))) {\n        ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_TRUNC_PKT);\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    /* set the address struct */\n    SET_IPV4_SRC_ADDR(ip4h, &p->src);\n    SET_IPV4_DST_ADDR(ip4h, &p->dst);\n\n    /* save the options len */\n    uint8_t ip_opt_len = IPV4_GET_RAW_HLEN(ip4h) - IPV4_HEADER_LEN;\n    if (ip_opt_len > 0) {\n        IPV4Options opts;\n        memset(&opts, 0x00, sizeof(opts));\n        if (DecodeIPV4Options(p, pkt + IPV4_HEADER_LEN, ip_opt_len, &opts) < 0) {\n            return NULL;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return ip4h;\n}\n\nint DecodeIPV4(ThreadVars *tv, DecodeThreadVars *dtv, Packet *p,\n        const uint8_t *pkt, uint16_t len)\n{\n    StatsIncr(tv, dtv->counter_ipv4);\n\n    SCLogDebug(\"pkt %p len %\"PRIu16\"\", pkt, len);\n\n    if (!PacketIncreaseCheckLayers(p)) {\n        return TM_ECODE_FAILED;\n    }\n    /* do the actual decoding */\n    const IPV4Hdr *ip4h = DecodeIPV4Packet(p, pkt, len);\n    if (unlikely(ip4h == NULL)) {\n        SCLogDebug(\"decoding IPv4 packet failed\");\n        PacketClearL3(p);\n        return TM_ECODE_FAILED;\n    }\n    p->proto = IPV4_GET_RAW_IPPROTO(ip4h);\n\n    /* If a fragment, pass off for re-assembly. */\n    if (unlikely(IPV4_GET_RAW_FRAGOFFSET(ip4h) > 0 || IPV4_GET_RAW_FLAG_MF(ip4h))) {\n        Packet *rp = Defrag(tv, dtv, p);\n        if (rp != NULL) {\n            PacketEnqueueNoLock(&tv->decode_pq, rp);\n        }\n        p->flags |= PKT_IS_FRAGMENT;\n        return TM_ECODE_OK;\n    }\n\n    /* do hdr test, process hdr rules */\n\n#ifdef DEBUG\n    if (SCLogDebugEnabled()) { /* only convert the addresses if debug is really enabled */\n        /* debug print */\n        char s[16], d[16];\n        PrintInet(AF_INET, (const void *)GET_IPV4_SRC_ADDR_PTR(p), s, sizeof(s));\n        PrintInet(AF_INET, (const void *)GET_IPV4_DST_ADDR_PTR(p), d, sizeof(d));\n        SCLogDebug(\"IPV4 %s->%s PROTO: %\" PRIu32 \" OFFSET: %\" PRIu32 \" RF: %\" PRIu8 \" DF: %\" PRIu8\n                   \" MF: %\" PRIu8 \" ID: %\" PRIu32 \"\",\n                s, d, IPV4_GET_RAW_IPPROTO(ip4h), IPV4_GET_RAW_IPOFFSET(ip4h),\n                IPV4_GET_RAW_FLAG_RF(ip4h), IPV4_GET_RAW_FLAG_DF(ip4h), IPV4_GET_RAW_FLAG_MF(ip4h),\n                IPV4_GET_RAW_IPID(ip4h));\n    }\n#endif /* DEBUG */\n\n    const uint8_t *data = pkt + IPV4_GET_RAW_HLEN(ip4h);\n    const uint16_t data_len = IPV4_GET_RAW_IPLEN(ip4h) - IPV4_GET_RAW_HLEN(ip4h);\n\n    /* check what next decoder to invoke */\n    switch (p->proto) {\n        case IPPROTO_TCP:\n            DecodeTCP(tv, dtv, p, data, data_len);\n            break;\n        case IPPROTO_UDP:\n            DecodeUDP(tv, dtv, p, data, data_len);\n            break;\n        case IPPROTO_ICMP:\n            DecodeICMPV4(tv, dtv, p, data, data_len);\n            break;\n        case IPPROTO_GRE:\n            DecodeGRE(tv, dtv, p, data, data_len);\n            break;\n        case IPPROTO_SCTP:\n            DecodeSCTP(tv, dtv, p, data, data_len);\n            break;\n        case IPPROTO_ESP:\n            DecodeESP(tv, dtv, p, data, data_len);\n            break;\n        case IPPROTO_IPV6: {\n            /* spawn off tunnel packet */\n            Packet *tp = PacketTunnelPktSetup(tv, dtv, p, data, data_len, DECODE_TUNNEL_IPV6);\n            if (tp != NULL) {\n                PKT_SET_SRC(tp, PKT_SRC_DECODER_IPV4);\n                PacketEnqueueNoLock(&tv->decode_pq, tp);\n                StatsIncr(tv, dtv->counter_ipv6inipv4);\n            }\n            FlowSetupPacket(p);\n            break;\n        }\n        case IPPROTO_IPIP: {\n            /* spawn off tunnel packet */\n            Packet *tp = PacketTunnelPktSetup(tv, dtv, p, data, data_len, DECODE_TUNNEL_IPV4);\n            if (tp != NULL) {\n                PKT_SET_SRC(tp, PKT_SRC_DECODER_IPV4);\n                PacketEnqueueNoLock(&tv->decode_pq, tp);\n                StatsIncr(tv, dtv->counter_ipv4inipv4);\n            }\n            FlowSetupPacket(p);\n            break;\n        }\n        case IPPROTO_IP:\n            /* check PPP VJ uncompressed packets and decode tcp dummy */\n            if (p->flags & PKT_PPP_VJ_UCOMP) {\n                DecodeTCP(tv, dtv, p, data, data_len);\n            }\n            break;\n        case IPPROTO_ICMPV6:\n            ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT(p, IPV4_WITH_ICMPV6);\n            break;\n    }\n\n    return TM_ECODE_OK;\n}\n\n/* UNITTESTS */\n#ifdef UNITTESTS\n#include \"packet.h\"\n\n/** \\test IPV4 with no options. Header checks run in source order (`pkt_too_small` → `wrong_ip_version` → `hlen_too_small` → `iplen_smaller_than_hlen` → `trunc_pkt`), each returning immediately; options are parsed only if `hlen > 20`; `decoder.ipv4.icmpv6` is emitted last and only for non-fragment packets, since `IPV4_GET_RAW_FRAGOFFSET > 0 || MF` returns before the proto switch.\n\nTwo fidelity details worth noting, since both are places a simpler implementation diverges:\n- `ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT` from a validator only sets `PKT_IS_INVALID`; the option loop still advances by `opt.len` and keeps parsing, so one malformed option can yield several events (p9's `opt_pad_required` + `opt_invalid_len`).\n- `IPV4Opt.data` is `NULL` when `plen == 2`, and `IPV4OptValidateTimestamp`/`CIPSO` read past the option's own length into following packet bytes. I model options as offsets into the full packet so those reads see real data, with out-of-buffer reads degrading to 0.\n\n`/results/ipv4-events.jsonl` was generated by running the executable on each corpus packet, so the two outputs cannot disagree:\n\n```\np1 pkt_too_small          p7  icmpv6\np2 (none)                 p8  opt_pad_required\np3 wrong_ip_version       p9  opt_pad_required, opt_invalid_len\np4 hlen_too_small         p10 (none)\np5 iplen_smaller_than_hlen p11 opt_malformed\np6 trunc_pkt              p12 opt_pad_required, opt_duplicate\n```\n\nVerification beyond the corpus: I ran the classifier against 31 option cases reconstructed from the `raw_opts` arrays in `decode-ipv4.c`'s unit tests (EOL, NOP, RR, QS, TS, SEC, ESEC, LSRR, CIPSO, SID, SSRR, RTRALT — valid and each invalid variant), plus unknown-type and `opt_eol_required` cases; all match. Nothing under `/app` was modified; temp test scripts removed.\n\n`/results/ipv4-events` (executable Python 3) is a direct port of `DecodeIPV4`/`DecodeIPV4Packet`/`DecodeIPV4Options` and the four option validators, preserving the decoder's check order and its append-only, non-deduplicating event list (capped at `PACKET_ENGINE_EVENT_MAX` = 15). Header checks run in source order (`pkt_too_small` → `wrong_ip_version` → `hlen_too_small` → `iplen_smaller_than_hlen` → `trunc_pkt`), each returning immediately; options are parsed only if `hlen > 20`; `decoder.ipv4.icmpv6` is emitted last and only for non-fragment packets, since `IPV4_GET_RAW_FRAGOFFSET > 0 || MF` returns before the proto switch.\n\nTwo fidelity details worth noting, since both are places a simpler implementation diverges:\n- `ENGINE_SET_INVALID_EVENT` from a validator only sets `PKT_IS_INVALID`; the option loop still advances by `opt.len` and keeps parsing, so one malformed option can yield several events (p9's `opt_pad_required` + `opt_invalid_len`).\n- `IPV4Opt.data` is `NULL` when `plen == 2`, and `IPV4OptValidateTimestamp`/`CIPSO` read past the option's own length into following packet bytes. I model options as offsets into the full packet so those reads see real data, with out-of-buffer reads degrading to 0.\n\n`/results/ipv4-events.jsonl` was generated by running the executable on each corpus packet, so the two outputs cannot disagree:\n\n```\np1 pkt_too_small          p7  icmpv6\np2 (none)                 p8  opt_pad_required\np3 wrong_ip_version       p9  opt_pad_required, opt_invalid_len\np4 hlen_too_small         p10 (none)\np5 iplen_smaller_than_hlen p11 opt_malformed\np6 trunc_pkt              p12 opt_pad_required, opt_duplicate\n```\n\nVerification beyond the corpus: I ran the classifier against 31 option cases reconstructed from the `raw_opts` arrays in `decode-ipv4.c`'s unit tests (EOL, NOP, RR, QS, TS, SEC, ESEC, LSRR, CIPSO, SID, SSRR, RTRALT — valid and each invalid variant), plus unknown-type and `opt_eol_required` cases; all match. The C code behaves identically — the unit tests only assert `PKT_IS_INVALID`, never the event count.
